- `2026_05_SF_SEA::SEA_SIDE` (same, strong (qualitative)): SEA moneyline, SEA spread, SEA team total over, SEA player props over — Team-side markets share the same game-script risk.
- `2026_05_SF_SEA::GAME_SCRIPT_OPPOSED` (opposed, moderate (qualitative)): favourite rushing volume over, favourite passing volume over — A team that leads runs more and throws less; volume props on the same team can conflict.
